RESULT    : PASS  |  best cost: 4.617e+06 area_delay_product  (eval 8)

[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `context/starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT of the baseline JPEG decoder
// (ultraembedded/core_jpeg, Apache 2.0): the verbatim composition of

// -> jpeg_idct_y, exactly as instantiated in the original jpeg_idct.v

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),
    .outport_idx_o(outport_idx)
  );

  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  // ---- load vectors.dat: flat token stream, 128 tokens per block ----------
  localparam MAX_BLOCKS = 1024;
  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],

... (showing lines 1-147 of 169; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
  -> read_file(filename='starting_point.v', offset=147, limit=700)

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 3/60]
    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C4_16 = 2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C6_16 = 1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C7_16 = 799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram0
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram1
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr1_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data1_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram2
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr2_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data2_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram3
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr3_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data3_w)
);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;

module jpeg_idct_y
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Params
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15
    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Ports
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C4_16 = 2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C5_16 = 2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C6_16 = 1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C7_16 = 799;  // cos(7pi/16) x4096

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

[ASSISTANT]
The baseline passes with cost 9.50M (area 4274 × delay 2222ps — the delay is dominated by the transpose RAM read mux). I now understand the original structure in detail. My plan:

1. **Rewrite the 1-D passes** with narrowed datapath widths (provable bounds: 16-bit inputs → products fit 29 bits, accumulators 31 bits), reduce from 5 to 4 time-shared multipliers (beat-4 reload is redundant; move the `i0*C4` load to beat 2), and replace the expensive signed `/256` divider with an exact truncation-toward-zero shift-add.
2. **Replace the transpose RAMs** (4× dual-port 32×32 with 32:1 read muxes — the critical path) with 8 row-banks × 2 block-copies of 8-deep shift registers: writes push sequentially, reads pop sequentially — no decode muxes at all, and 20-bit storage instead of 32-bit.

Let me write the design:
  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT of the baseline JPEG decoder\n// (ultraembedded/core_jpeg fixed-point behavior, exactly reproduced).\n//\n// Optimized implementation:\n//  - 1-D passes (AAN-style factorization, constants x4096): 4 time-shared\n//    multipliers over an 8-beat schedule, narrowed datapath widths.\n//    All bound-provable arithmetic done in minimal width; the 32-bit\n//    wrap points of the original (x*181 product) are preserved exactly.\n//  - (x*181)/256 : signed division truncating toward zero implemented\n//    as ((p<0 ? p+255 : p) >>> 8) — no divider.\n//  - Corner turn: 8 row-banks x 2 block-copies of 8-deep shift registers.\n//    Writes push in row order, reads pop in column order: no address\n//    decode, no read muxes.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_1d #(\n    parameter integer OUT_SHIFT = 11,\n    parameter integer IN_W      = 16,\n    parameter integer A_W       = 18,\n    parameter integer PROD_W    = 29,\n    parameter integer T_W       = 31,\n    parameter integer OUT_W     = 20,\n    parameter integer IDX_SWAP  = 0\n)(\n    input  wire             clk,\n    input  wire             rst,\n    input  wire             img_start_i,\n    input  wire             inport_valid_i,\n    input  wire [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data0_i,\n    input  wire [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data1_i,\n    input  wire [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data2_i,\n    input  wire [IN_W-1:0]  inport_data3_i,\n    input  wire [2:0]       inport_idx_i,\n    output wire             outport_valid_o,\n    output wire [OUT_W-1:0] outport_data_o,\n    output wire [5:0]       outport_idx_o\n);\n\n    localparam signed [12:0] C1_16 = 13'sd4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C2_16 = 13'sd3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C3_16 = 13'sd3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C4_16 = 13'sd2896; // cos(4pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C5_16 = 13'sd2276; // cos(5pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C6_16 = 13'sd1567; // cos(6pi/16) x4096\n    localparam signed [12:0] C7_16 = 13'sd799; m0b <= C2_16;\n                m1a  <= bin3; m1b <= C6_16;\n            end\n            3'd1: begin\n                m0a  <= bin0; m0b <= C1_16;\n                m1a  <= bin3; m1b <= C7_16;\n                m2a  <= bin2; m2b <= C5_16;\n                m3a  <= bin1; m3b <= C3_16;\n            end\n            3'd2: begin\n                i0_r <= bin0 - bin2;\n                m3a  <= i0_r; m3b <= C4_16; // (x0+x4)*C4, i0 still old value\n            end\n            3'd3: begin\n                m0a  <= bin0; m0b <= C7_16;\n                m1a  <= bin3; m1b <= C1_16;\n                m2a  <= bin2; m2b <= C3_16;\n                m3a  <= bin1; m3b <= C5_16;\n            end\n            // 3'd4: products of beat 3 persist, no reload needed\n            3'd5: begin\n                m0a  <= bin1; m0b <= C6_16;\n                m1a  <= bin3; m1b <= C2_16;\n                m3a  <= i0_r; m3b <= C4_16; // (x0-x4)*C4\n            end\n            default: ;\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    //-----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Multipliers (2-stage pipeline)\n    //-----------------------------------------------------------------\n    reg signed [PROD_W-1:0] p0_q, p1_q, p2_q, p3_q;\n    reg signed [PROD_W-1:0] p0, p1, p2, p3;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            p0_q <= {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n            p1_q <= {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n            p2_q <= {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n            p3_q <= {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n        end else begin\n            p0_q <= m0a * m0b;\n            p1_q <= m1a * m1b;\n            p2_q <= m2a * m2b;\n            p3_q <= m3a * m3b;\n        end\n    end\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            p0 <= {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n            p1 <= {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n            p2 <= {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n            p3 <= {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n        end else begin\n            p0 <= p0_q;\n            p1 <= p1_q;\n            p2 <= p2_q;\n            p3 <= p3_q;\n        end\n    end\n\n    //-----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Pipeline valid/idx\n    //-----------------------------------------------------------------\n    reg       v0, v1, v2, v3;\n    reg [2:0] d0, d1, d2, d3;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            v0 <= 1'b0; // s3\n            end\n            3'd1: begin\n                os7 <= p0 + p1;\n                os6 <= p2 + p3;\n            end\n            3'd2: begin\n                ot0 <= p3 + ot3; // t0 = s0 + s3\n                ot3 <= p3 - ot3; // t3 = s0 - s3\n                ot7 <= os6 + os7;\n            end\n            3'd3: begin\n                ot4 <= (p0 - p1) + (p2 - p3);\n            end\n            3'd4: begin\n                ot0 <= p0 - p1; // s4\n                os5 <= p2 - p3;\n            end\n            3'd5: begin\n                ot3 <= p0 - p1; // s2\n                ot4 <= p3;      // s1\n                ot5 <= ot0 - os5;\n                ot6 <= os7 - os6;\n            end\n            3'd6: begin\n                ot1  <= ot4 + ot3;\n                ot2  <= ot4 - ot3;\n                ot65 <= ot6 - ot5;\n                ot56 <= ot5 + ot6;\n            end\n            default: begin\n                os5 <= $signed(div_q_5[24:0]); // 1/sqrt(2)\n                os6 <= $signed(div_q_6[24:0]); // 1/sqrt(2)\n            end\n            endcase\n        end\n    end\n\n    //-----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Output stage\n    //-----------------------------------------------------------------\n    wire signed [31:0] sum0 = ot0 + ot7;\n    wire signed [31:0] sum7 = ot0 - ot7;\n    wire signed [31:0] sum3 = ot3 + ot4;\n    wire signed [31:0] sum4 = ot3 - ot4;\n    wire signed [31:0] sum2 = ot2 + os5;\n    wire signed [31:0] sum5 = ot2 - os5;\n    wire signed [31:0] sum1 = ot1 + os6;\n    wire signed [31:0] sum6 = ot1 - os6;\n\n    wire signed [31:0] sh0 = sum0 >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    wire signed [31:0] sh7 = sum7 >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    wire signed [31:0] sh3 = sum3 >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    wire signed [31:0] sh4 = sum4 >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    wire signed [31:0] sh2 = sum2 >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    wire signed [31:0] sh5 = sum5 >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    wire signed [31:0] sh1 = sum1 >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n    wire signed [31:0] sh6 = sum6 >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n\n    reg signed [OUT_W-1:0] bo0, bo1, bo2, bo3, bo4, bo5, bo6, bo7;\n    reg signed [OUT_W-1:0] bo_tmp;\n\n    always @(posedge clk) begin\n        if (rst) begin\n            bo0 <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo1 <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo2 <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo3 <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo4 <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo5 <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo6 <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo7 <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n            bo_tmp <= {OUT_W{1'b0}};\n        end else if (v3) begin\n            if (d3 == 3'd3) begin\n                bo0    <= sh0[OUT_W-1:0];\n                bo_tmp <= sh7[OUT_W-1:0]; b < 8; b = b + 1) begin : g_en\n            assign wr_en0[b] = inport_valid_i & ~wr_copy_q & (inport_idx_i[5:3] == b);\n            assign wr_en1[b] = inport_valid_i &  wr_copy_q & (inport_idx_i[5:3] == b);\n            assign rd_en0[b] = rd_active_q &  wr_copy_q & (rd_parity_w == (b % 2));\n            assign rd_en1[b] = rd_active_q & ~wr_copy_q & (rd_parity_w == (b % 2));\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // [copy][bank][stage];               end\n        end\n    end\n\n    // read taps (oldest entry of each bank)\n    wire [DW-1:0] tap [0:15];\n    generate\n        for (b = 0; b < 8; b = b + 1) begin : g_tap\n ... (showing lines 147-169 of 169; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
The original is now clear. Key insight from tracing the original's transpose: each read address is re-read multiple times per column (the y-pass re-uses coefficients across beats), so a pure FIFO won't work with the original y-pass — unless the y-pass captures the delivered values into registers at their first use (beats j0/j1), after which all loads read registers. That makes the corner-turn deliverable by plain shift registers with **no read muxes** (the dominant cost/delay in the baseline).

Let me verify my understanding of the original's pipeline alignment is right by re-reading the load/consume stages once more:
  -> read_file(filename='starting_point.v', offset=186, limit=120)

  -> replace_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT of the baseline JPEG decoder\n// (ultraembedded/core_jpeg fixed-point behavior, exactly reproduced).\n//\n// Optimized implementation:\n//  - 1-D passes (AAN-style factorization, constants x4096): 4 time-shared\n//    multipliers over an 8-beat schedule (the original's beat-4 multiplier\n//    reload is redundant), narrowed datapath widths (bound-provable).\n//  - (x*181)/256 with signed truncation toward zero, implemented as\n//    ((p<0 ? p+255 : p) >>> 8) — no divider, 32-bit wrap on x*181 kept.\n//  - Corner turn: 8 row-banks x 2 block-copies of 8-deep shift registers.\n//    Writes push in row order, reads pop in column order: no address\n//    decode, no read muxes. [ASSISTANT]
The alignment analysis is complete. Two real bugs to fix in my transpose: (1) the pop pattern must deliver each bank value **once per column** (even banks pop at beat j=0, odd banks at j=1), with the read tap *holding* the value across the column's beats so the y-pass can re-read it at beats j2/j3/j5; (2) the read must trigger on the *just-completed* copy (the last block has no successor to trigger its read). Writing the corrected design:

Replaced file: design.v

[ASSISTANT]
Now I have the complete picture of the original's pipeline alignment (verified by tracing stage delays: load at beat j → product reg at j+1 → accumulator case m at cycle m+3 reading products loaded at beat m+1 → output stage at delay 4 → streamer at delay 7 more). Key realizations for my redesign:

1. **The 2nd product register stage is redundant** — one product register still lands `product(beat m+1)` in the accumulator's case-m cycle, identically to the original.
2. **The beat-4 multiplier reload is redundant for the x-pass** (beats 3 and 4 deliver identical lane data), but **not for the y-pass** (beat 3 delivers odd-row values, beat 4 even-row values).
3. The original transpose delivers, per column `c`: odd-row set at beats {0,2,3,5}, even-row set at {1,4,6}. That means each bank's value must be *held* visible across several beats — achievable with one pop per bank per column (odd banks pop at beat 5, even at beat 6), and the push sequence naturally leaves value 0 at the read tap when reading starts. No address decode, no 32:1 muxes.

Writing the full design:

wr_copy_q   <= 1'b0;\n            rd_copy_q   <= 1'b0;\n            rd_active_q <= 1'b0;\n            rd_cnt_q    <= 6'd0;\n        end else if (wr_done_w) begin\n            wr_copy_q   <= [TOOL RESULT]
[Step 12/60]
Created file: design.v

[ASSISTANT]
Now I have the complete, verified picture of the original's timing (I traced every stage delay and the exact transpose read-address sequence). Key findings that enable big savings:

1. **The original's beat→data mapping**: transpose delivers the **even row set at beats {0,2,5,7}** and odd set at {1,3,4,6}; each bank value is re-read across beats — so a shift-register bank that **holds its tap and pops once per column** reproduces it exactly, with **no address decode and no 32:1 read muxes** (the baseline's critical path).
2. **Case m consumes products loaded at beat m** (3-deep index pipeline matches the 3-stage product chain) → the second product register is droppable if the accumulator uses the 2-stage-delayed index.
3. **4 multipliers suffice**: move `(x0+x4)·C4` to beat 0 (case 0 writes s0), `(x0−x4)·C4` to beat 5 via lane 2, and skip the redundant beat-4 reload (beats 3 and 4 deliver identical data in *both* passes).

Writing the full design:
  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — full 8x8 2-D IDCT of the baseline JPEG decoder\n// (ultraembedded/core_jpeg fixed-point behavior, exactly reproduced).\n//\n// Optimized implementation:\n//  - 1-D passes (AAN-style factorization, constants x4096): 4 time-shared\n//    constant-per-beat multiplier lanes (the original uses 5: the s0\n//    product (x0+x4)*C4 is produced by lane 2 loaded at beat 0 and\n//    consumed by accumulator case 0; the (x0-x4)*C4 product is produced\n//    by lane 2 at beat 5; the beat-4 operand reload of the original is\n//    redundant since beat 3 and beat 4 deliver identical data in both\n//    passes). beats (column c = cnt[5:3], beat j = cnt[2:0]).\n// Odd banks (rows 1,3,5,7) pop at j==5, even banks (rows 0,2,4,6) at j==6;\n// between pops the read tap holds, reproducing the original delivery\n// [ASSISTANT]
**PASS — cost 5.29M (from 9.50M).** Area 3702, delay 1430ps. The critical path is now the `(x*181)/256` divider chain (multiply + correction + case-mux) in the x-pass. Next optimizations:

1. **Split the divider**: register the `*181` product at beat-7, do the sign-fixup inside the output stage (which fires one cycle later) — removes the multiply from the mux path.
2. **Restructure the accumulator into single-write registers** (no 8-way case muxes — each value gets a dedicated reg + enable).
3. Simplify transpose pops (all banks pop at one beat), drop the unneeded memory reset.

[ASSISTANT]
The failures show my re-timing introduced subtle misalignments. Rather than continue patching, I'll revert to the known-good eval-2 alignment (accumulator `case(d1)`, output stage `case(d2)`/`v3`) and apply the ÷256 split **without** moving the output stage: register the `*181` products at **case 6** (computing them directly from the `t5`/`t6` datapath being written that cycle), leaving only the fixup+shift in case 7. Combined with the single-write accumulator (which eliminates all case muxes via dedicated enables), this shortens both critical paths:

The timing sweep confirms 500ps is optimal for this structure. ## Summary

**Final result: PASS, cost 4.617M area_delay_product** (baseline starting point: 9.50M — a 2.06× improvement). Best design is `design.v` (confirmed in eval_8/eval_11); area 3897, delay 1184ps.

### 1. Approaches tried
- **Baseline eval** (`starting_point.v`): 9.50M (area 4274, delay 2222ps — critical path was the transpose RAM read mux).
- **Full rewrite** (`design.v`): 1-D passes with narrowed, bound-provable widths; corner-turn rebuilt from 4 dual-port 32×32 RAMs into 8 row-banks × 2 block-copies of 8-deep **shift registers**; ÷256 divider replaced by sign-fixup + arithmetic shift; accumulator de-muxed into one dedicated register + enable per value; `*181` products registered one cycle early. → 4.62M.
- **2-stage split multipliers** (low/hi partial products, shifted pipeline): 5.34M — **worse**, reverted.
- **Constant-coefficient multipliers** with per-beat operand muxes (`design2.v`): FAIL — beat-mux timing error (operand select and product register used misaligned beat enables).
- **target_delay sweep**: 500ps → 4.62M; 1000ps → identical 4.62M; 1400ps → 4.93M. Low constraint is optimal.

### 2. Most impactful optimizations
1. **Shift-register transpose** (no address decode, no 32:1 read mux): eliminated the original's worst timing path, dropping delay 2222→~1430ps. Key insight: the original re-reads each RAM word across several beats, so a bank that pops once per column and *holds* its tap reproduces the delivery pattern exactly.
2. **Width narrowing** (16→18-bit A path, 29-bit products, 31-bit accumulators, 20-bit x-out): big area win while preserving every 32-bit wrap point.
3. **Dropping the 5th multiplier** (move `(x0±x4)·C4` to other lanes; beat-4 reload is redundant since beats 3/4 carry identical data) and the **redundant second product register**.
4. **Divider elimination** and de-muxed accumulator.

### 3. Regressions/failures
- **Register-reuse bugs**: the original reuses `o_t0` (overwritten by s4 at case 4, so `t5 = s4 − s5`, not `t0 − s5`) and `o_t4/o_t3` (case 6 computes `s1 ± s2`, not `t4 ± s2`). My split registers exposed the true dataflow; three eval cycles were lost to these.
- **Re-timing the ÷256 across the output stage** caused misalignment twice; the working form registers `*181` in case 6 (same cycle the operands are written) and does fixup+shift in case 7.
- **2-stage multiplier split** hurt: the y-pass operand-load path (transpose tap → muxed operand regs) became critical.

### 4. Lessons
- Trace the *original* pipeline delays and register-reuse aliasing precisely before restructuring — the two register-reuse subtleties cost more steps than any optimization.
- Keep a known-good checkpoint and make one structural change per eval; reverts were cheap and reliable.
- Structural changes (mux elimination) dominate; `target_delay` is a minor knob. If more steps remained: fix the beat-mux alignment in `design2.v` (constant multipliers could cut both area and delay), and narrow the y-pass accumulator widths (sums fit ~35 bits but case muxes still use 32-bit wiring).
